docs/design/excavation-command-execution.md:43:12. Cut pattern определяется типом target, а не текущей work position: vertical front-slice tunnel всегда удаляет горизонтальные строки `UpperLeft+UpperRight`, затем `LowerLeft+LowerRight`; horizontal tunnel использует ближайшую вертикальную колонку; depth target использует отдельный depth-face pattern. Смена work cell не меняет cut pattern.
docs/design/excavation-command-execution.md:45:14. Свободные residents независимо выбирают свои ближайшие доступные jobs; один job/work position не назначается двум residents.
docs/design/excavation-command-execution.md:46:15. После каждого World quarter commit work position и support переоцениваются. Любой completed quarter в клетке непосредственно под resident отменяет full standing support: runtime выбирает доступную side/depth work cell либо немедленно переводит resident в stationary climbing stance спиной к камере.
docs/design/excavation-command-execution.md:49:18. Для depth target из vertical tunnel work position выбирается в таком порядке: достижимая открытая horizontal cell слева/справа на source depth; затем достижимая открытая depth cell слева/справа от target; затем сама vertical source cell с climbing-work stance. Один выбранный work cell остаётся authoritative position reservation job.
docs/design/excavation-command-execution.md:55:Несколько spatial jobs могут иметь одну и ту же или одинаково удалённую work position. Это не делает более глубокую/нижнюю target cell равной верхней: первичным остаётся расстояние от resident до самой target cell. Поэтому при копке сверху вниз верхняя правая/левая клетка выбирается раньше нижней клетки, если она ближе к resident и достижима.

Это правило не ограничено direct order.

docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:1:# Campfire cooking and direct food use
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:10:- [`mushroom-growth-and-chopping.md`](mushroom-growth-and-chopping.md);
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:11:- [`content/food.md`](content/food.md);
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:18:This specification owns the vertical workflow that turns a queued campfire food order into a world food item and lets a selected resident pick it up or eat it directly. It composes the existing Production, BuildingSupply, Mushroom, Inventory, Jobs, AgentNeeds and contextual-input owners; it must not create duplicate inventories, nutrition state or Unity-only job truth.
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:23:- recipe: `recipe.campfire.food.grilled_mushroom`;
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:24:- input: `material.mushroom_cap`;
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:25:- output: `food.grilled_mushroom`;
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:30:One order consumes exactly one mushroom cap from that campfire internal stock and creates one ordinary world stack containing two grilled mushrooms.
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:40:For every queued grilled-mushroom order the runtime evaluates one deterministic chain:
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:42:1. If the campfire internal stock contains one unreserved mushroom cap, prepare the production job immediately.
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:43:2. Otherwise, if a revealed, reachable, unreserved world mushroom cap exists, create the ordinary protected BuildingSupply job.
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:44:3. Otherwise, if a revealed and reachable `Large` mushroom exists without an active chop attempt, create exactly one ordinary mushroom-chop job as the dependency.
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:45:4. Mushroom drops remain world items. On following synchronization ticks the normal BuildingSupply planner reserves a cap, delivers it, and the normal production preparation starts.
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:49:At most one active dependency chop may be created per blocked campfire order. Existing world caps always take priority over creating another chop.
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:53:The resident assigned to `ProductionWorkJob` remains the authoritative worker through `Finalize`. That worker performs output placement; no anonymous completion adapter or second hauling job creates the food.
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:62:If no valid surrounding cell exists, the order remains ready to complete with `production.output_space_unavailable`. Retry must not consume another cap, duplicate output, repeat progression, or lose the assigned completion owner.
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:66:When at least one living resident is selected and the pointer is over an available `food.grilled_mushroom` world stack:
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:68:- without `Alt`, the cursor is the existing animated upward pickup arrow; LMB creates the existing direct `WorldItemPickup` job for the selected resident;
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:69:- with `Alt`, the cursor is an animated green mouth; `Alt+LMB` creates the same authoritative approach/pickup job plus a post-pickup eat intent;
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:71:- a failed or cancelled pickup clears the post-pickup eat intent and does not consume food;
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:72:- after successful pickup, the exact carried food unit starts the authoritative meal action immediately.
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:74:BuildingBox input and ordinary non-food item routing are unchanged.
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:78:Direct and autonomous food use share one Agent meal owner.
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:80:A grilled mushroom portion provides 15 Nutrition points (`1500` fixed-point units) over exactly three bites. The portion is removed from Inventory at meal start. Each completed bite applies exactly one third (`500`) to Nutrition. The first bite is the start of consumption for history/diagnostics. Interruption preserves already-applied bites and loses the consumed remainder; restart requires another portion.
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:87:- One world cap or food stack cannot feed two jobs.
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:88:- Different residents may harvest, deliver, cook, pick up and eat concurrently when they own different entities/jobs.
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:90:- If the selected resident has no compatible free inventory slot, direct pickup/eat is rejected before job creation and the food remains in the world.
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:94:Existing production, supply, mushroom and pickup job codecs retain their authority. The post-pickup action is stored in `WorldItemPickupJobDefinition` and its codec. Save format v9 stores resident needs, active food item, original meal start tick, completed bite count and remaining bite plan.
docs/design/campfire-cooking-and-food-use.md:96:Loading restores the exact holder and completed bite progress without replaying pickup, consuming another portion, applying completed Nutrition again, repeating progression or output completion. Older saves migrate to an empty agent-runtime section and therefore load with no active meal. Older pickup-job payloads without `completion_action` decode as `None`.

docs/design/resident-movement-modes.md:13:Система выбирает один типизированный режим для каждого authoritative cell transition resident. docs/design/resident-movement-modes.md:49:## 5. Repeat policy
docs/design/resident-movement-modes.md:51:Repeat не использует wall clock, frame time или приблизительное расстояние. Команда является повторной, когда новый manual route заменяет уже активный manual route того же resident и имеет тот же конечный `CellId`.
docs/design/resident-movement-modes.md:54:- та же цель публикует `RepeatedCommand` и включает forced-repeat policy;
docs/design/resident-movement-modes.md:55:- replan сохраняет repeat flag;
docs/design/resident-movement-modes.md:56:- завершённый route больше не является repeat source.
docs/design/resident-movement-modes.md:71:Resolver поддерживает `Reithamster` и `Hoverboard`, forced-repeat и nullable automatic long-route policy. Production activation остаётся выключенной до появления stable item IDs/runtime definitions и утверждённых Q-014 values. Это validation boundary, а не fallback к строковым именам или скрытым magic coefficients.
docs/design/resident-movement-modes.md:79:- `RepeatedCommand`;

docs/implementation/runtime-screenshot-barrel-mushroom-stock-regression-2026-07-28.md:26:### Mushroom execution stall
docs/implementation/runtime-screenshot-barrel-mushroom-stock-regression-2026-07-28.md:28:Mushroom Domain/Application workflow не был первопричиной. `DesignateTunnelDepth` создавал spatial excavation job без предварительной authoritative `CellDesignation.Dig`. Дополнительно erase/stale paths не всегда отменяли spatial job и не очищали quarter coordinator. Поздний quarter commit поэтому мог попасть в solid undesignated cell и выбросить исключение внутри общего simulation loop до mushroom advancement.

docs/implementation/navigation.md:111:3. creates a grounded corridor;
docs/implementation/navigation.md:131:`TunnelNavigationVolume` projects typed edges instead of treating every same-Y open neighbor as ordinary floor walking: `SupportedWalk`, `VerticalClimb`, `ShaftGapTraverse`, and `DepthTraverse`. Route selection uses a deterministic lexicographic cost that minimizes `ShaftGapTraverse` count before total steps and `CellId`; an available depth-layer detour therefore wins over walking directly across an unsupported vertical shaft. When no detour exists, the horizontal shaft-gap edge remains traversable but Presentation receives its climbing kind and renders the resident facing away from the camera. World partial-excavation support participates in the same projection, so a worker cannot retain standing posture after the supporting cell loses a quarter.

docs/implementation/mushroom-finalization-and-barrel-surface-route-regression-2026-07-28.md:1:# Mushroom finalization and barrel surface-route regression
docs/implementation/mushroom-finalization-and-barrel-surface-route-regression-2026-07-28.md:7:- [`../design/mushroom-growth-and-chopping.md`](../design/mushroom-growth-and-chopping.md), tracking [#423](https://github.com/bageus/Dig/issues/423);
docs/implementation/mushroom-finalization-and-barrel-surface-route-regression-2026-07-28.md:11:## Mushroom final-swing root cause
docs/implementation/mushroom-finalization-and-barrel-surface-route-regression-2026-07-28.md:13:`CompleteMushroomSwingCommandHandler` already advances the authoritative chopping job from `PerformWork` to `Finalize` when the last required swing completes. The Unity adapter continued using its stale pre-swing `JobSnapshot` and called the generic `AdvanceJobHandler` a second time. That moved the job from `Finalize` to terminal `Completed` before `CompleteMushroomChopCommand` could atomically transition the site to `AbsentRegrowing`, create cap/leg units and apply the exactly-once Woodworking grant.
docs/implementation/mushroom-finalization-and-barrel-surface-route-regression-2026-07-28.md:15:`AdvanceMushroomJob` now reloads the authoritative job after the final swing and commits `CompleteMushroomChopCommand` immediately when the reloaded stage is `Finalize`. The generic advance remains only for legacy/restored state where all swings are already stored while the job is still at `PerformWork`.
docs/implementation/mushroom-finalization-and-barrel-surface-route-regression-2026-07-28.md:17:`MushroomFinalSwingPlayModeTests` drives the actual Unity adapter through arrival and every swing, then requires a completed job, an `AbsentRegrowing` site and exactly two `material.mushroom_cap` plus one `material.mushroom_leg` unit for a Large mushroom.
docs/implementation/mushroom-finalization-and-barrel-surface-route-regression-2026-07-28.md:21:Barrel work-position selection and movement replanning accepted any successful `NavigationPathfinder` result to an adjacent walkable cell. Walkability alone includes shaft gaps, vertical/depth traversal and cells whose floor has been partially excavated, so a resident could visually approach through air and hit without a full standing plane.
docs/implementation/mushroom-finalization-and-barrel-surface-route-regression-2026-07-28.md:26:- the final adjacent work position to have full support;
docs/implementation/mushroom-finalization-and-barrel-surface-route-regression-2026-07-28.md:29:`VerticalClimb`, `ShaftGapTraverse`, `DepthTraverse`, unsupported cells and partially excavated support are rejected both during hover/command resolution and during later movement replanning. `BarrelAttackSurfacePlayModeTests` checks the supported/airborne path policy and verifies that a resolved demo attack position is adjacent to the barrel and stands over a full support cell.
docs/implementation/world-item-pickup-equipment.md:5:- `Alt + left click` on an available generic world item creates a typed pickup Job for the selected living resident.
